# tags: what the tagger reads of each sentence of tests/corpus/core/, one line a sentence.
# tests/golden.rs writes this file; `make fix-golden` rewrites it, and the diff must be read.
# A word is `text`, or `text/READING` once the tagger has read it. Any other token is its
# text, or [code], [html], [image], [url] or [footnote].
# READING is the tag code, then each feature set, from .s .p .1 .2 .3 .fin .inf .pp .ing .pres
# .past .pos .cmp .sup .c, then `:S`, `:L`, `:U` or `:?` for Sure, Likely,
# Unsure or Unknown, then `+CODE` for each other tag kept, as in runs/VERB.s.3.fin.pres:L+NOUN,
# then `@sym`, `@cmd`, `@path` or `@flag` if the word's origin is not English: foo_bar/PROPN.s:L+NOUN@sym
# deslag::tag::VERSION 11
# 33547 tokens, 25803 words, 25803 words with a reading
